===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence.whatsapp;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.WhatsAppPersistencePort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.WhatsAppContact;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.WhatsAppEvent;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.WhatsAppMessage;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence.WhatsAppEventRepository;
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class WhatsAppMongoAdapter implements WhatsAppPersistencePort {
    private final WhatsAppMessageRepository messageRepository;
    private final WhatsAppContactRepository contactRepository;
    private final WhatsAppEventRepository eventRepository;


    @Override
    public Mono<WhatsAppContact> saveContact(WhatsAppContact contact) {
        WhatsAppContactEntity entity = new WhatsAppContactEntity(
                contact.id(), contact.phoneNumber(), contact.name(),
                contact.preferredLanguage(), contact.tenantId()
        );
        return contactRepository.save(entity).map(e -> new WhatsAppContact(
                e.id(), e.phoneNumber(), e.name(), e.preferredLanguage(), e.tenantId()
        ));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<WhatsAppMessage> saveMessage(WhatsAppMessage message) {
        WhatsAppMessageEntity entity = new WhatsAppMessageEntity(
                message.id(), message.sender(), message.recipient(), message.originalText(),
                message.translatedText(), message.sourceLanguage(), message.targetLanguage(),
                message.timestamp(), message.tenantId(), message.outgoing(), message.translated(),
                message.status(), message.externalMessageId(), message.failureReason(), message.retryCount(),
                message.messageKind(), message.mediaUrl(), message.mimeType(), message.caption(), message.hasMedia()
        );
        return messageRepository.save(entity)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppMessage(
                        e.id(), e.sender(), e.recipient(), e.originalText(), e.translatedText(),
                        e.sourceLanguage(), e.targetLanguage(), e.timestamp(), e.tenantId(),
                        e.outgoing(), e.translated(), e.status(), e.externalMessageId(),
                        e.failureReason(), e.retryCount(),
                        e.messageKind() != null ? e.messageKind() : "TEXT",
                        e.mediaUrl(), e.mimeType(), e.caption(),
                        Boolean.TRUE.equals(e.hasMedia())
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<WhatsAppContact> findContactByPhoneAndTenant(String phoneNumber, String tenantId) {
        return contactRepository.findByPhoneNumberAndTenantId(phoneNumber, tenantId)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppContact(
                        e.id(), e.phoneNumber(), e.name(), e.preferredLanguage(), e.tenantId()
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Flux<WhatsAppMessage> findMessagesByTenant(String tenantId) {
        return messageRepository.findByTenantIdOrderByTimestampDesc(tenantId)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppMessage(
                        e.id(), e.sender(), e.recipient(), e.originalText(), e.translatedText(),
                        e.sourceLanguage(), e.targetLanguage(), e.timestamp(), e.tenantId(),
                        e.outgoing(), e.translated(), e.status(), e.externalMessageId(), e.failureReason(), e.retryCount(),
                        e.messageKind() != null ? e.messageKind() : "TEXT",
                        e.mediaUrl(), e.mimeType(), e.caption(),
                        Boolean.TRUE.equals(e.hasMedia())
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Flux<WhatsAppContact> findContactsByTenant(final String tenantId) {
        return contactRepository.findByTenantId(tenantId)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppContact(
                        e.id(), e.phoneNumber(), e.name(), e.preferredLanguage(), e.tenantId()
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Flux<WhatsAppMessage> findChatHistory(final String tenantId, final String phoneNumber) {
        return messageRepository.findConversationByTenantAndContact(tenantId, phoneNumber)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppMessage(
                        e.id(), e.sender(), e.recipient(), e.originalText(), e.translatedText(),
                        e.sourceLanguage(), e.targetLanguage(), e.timestamp(), e.tenantId(),
                        e.outgoing(), e.translated(), e.status(), e.externalMessageId(), e.failureReason(), e.retryCount(),
                        e.messageKind() != null ? e.messageKind() : "TEXT",
                        e.mediaUrl(), e.mimeType(), e.caption(),
                        Boolean.TRUE.equals(e.hasMedia())
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<WhatsAppEvent> saveEvent(WhatsAppEvent event) {
        WhatsAppEventEntity entity = new WhatsAppEventEntity(
                event.id(), event.tenantId(), event.eventType(), event.rawPayload(),
                event.receivedAt(), event.status(), event.processingError()
        );
        return eventRepository.save(entity)
                .map(e -> new WhatsAppEvent(
                        e.id(), e.tenantId(), e.eventType(), e.rawPayload(),
                        e.receivedAt(), e.status(), e.processingError()
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<Void> updateEventStatus(String eventId, String status, String error) {
        return eventRepository.findById(eventId)
                .flatMap(entity -> {
                    WhatsAppEventEntity updated = new WhatsAppEventEntity(
                            entity.id(), entity.tenantId(), entity.eventType(),
                            entity.rawPayload(), entity.receivedAt(), status, error
                    );
                    return eventRepository.save(updated);
                })
                .then();
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<WhatsAppMessage> findByExternalId(String tenantId, String externalId) {
        return messageRepository.findByTenantIdAndExternalMessageId(tenantId, externalId)
                .map(this::mapToDomain);
    }

    private WhatsAppMessage mapToDomain(WhatsAppMessageEntity e) {
        return new WhatsAppMessage(
                e.id(), e.sender(), e.recipient(), e.originalText(), e.translatedText(),
                e.sourceLanguage(), e.targetLanguage(), e.timestamp(), e.tenantId(),
                e.outgoing(), e.translated(), e.status(), e.externalMessageId(),
                e.failureReason(), e.retryCount(),
                e.messageKind() != null ? e.messageKind() : "TEXT",
                e.mediaUrl(), e.mimeType(), e.caption(),
                Boolean.TRUE.equals(e.hasMedia())
        );
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.config;

import com.mongodb.reactivestreams.client.MongoClient;
import com.mongodb.reactivestreams.client.MongoClients;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.config.AbstractReactiveMongoConfiguration;
import org.springframework.data.mongodb.repository.config.EnableReactiveMongoRepositories;

/**
 * Configuration class for Reactive MongoDB.
 */
@Configuration
@EnableReactiveMongoRepositories(
        basePackages = "pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence"
)
public class MongoConfig extends AbstractReactiveMongoConfiguration {

    @Value("${spring.data.mongodb.uri}")
    private String mongoUri;

    @Override
    protected String getDatabaseName() {
        return "fibertechia";
    }

    @Override
    @Bean
    public MongoClient reactiveMongoClient() {
        return MongoClients.create(mongoUri);

    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence.measurement;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.persistence.GarmentPersistencePort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.Garment;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.ProductMeasurementModel;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class GarmentMongoAdapter implements GarmentPersistencePort {

    private final GarmentRepository repository;

    @Override
    public Mono<Garment> findById(final String garmentId) {
        return repository.findById(garmentId)
                .map(entity -> new Garment(
                        entity.id(),
                        entity.category(),
                        entity.measurements() != null ? entity.measurements().stream()
                                .map(m -> new ProductMeasurementModel(
                                        m.size(),
                                        m.chestContour(),
                                        m.shoulderWidth(),
                                        m.totalLength(),
                                        m.sleeveLength()
                                ))
                                .collect(Collectors.toList()) : Collections.emptyList()
                ));
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence.measurement;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.persistence.MeasurementPersistencePort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.MeasurementHistory;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class MeasurementMongoAdapter implements MeasurementPersistencePort {

    private final MeasurementHistoryRepository repository;

    @Override
    public Mono<MeasurementHistory> save(final MeasurementHistory history) {
        final MeasurementHistoryEntity entity = new MeasurementHistoryEntity(
                history.id(),
                history.userId(),
                history.garmentId(),
                history.chest(),
                history.waist(),
                history.hips(),
                history.recommendedSize(),
                // >>> CHANGE
                history.matchSuccess(),
                history.explanation(),
                history.warningMessage(),
                // <<< CHANGE
                history.createdAt()
        );

        return repository.save(entity)
                .map(saved -> new MeasurementHistory(
                        saved.id(),
                        saved.userId(),
                        saved.garmentId(),
                        saved.chest(),
                        saved.waist(),
                        saved.hips(),
                        saved.recommendedSize(),
                        saved.matchSuccess(),
                        saved.explanation(),
                        saved.warningMessage(),
                        saved.createdAt()
                ));
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/OpenAiVisionAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClientResponseException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.UserMeasurementMetadata;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.AiMeasurementPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.BodyMeasurementResult;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
import reactor.util.retry.Retry;

import java.time.Duration;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OpenAiVisionAdapter implements AiMeasurementPort {

    private final WebClient.Builder webClientBuilder;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    @Value("${openai.api.key:default-secret-key}")
    private String openAiApiKey;

    private static final String OPENAI_URL = "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions";

    @Override
    public Mono<BodyMeasurementResult> extractMeasurements(
            final String systemPrompt,
            final UserMeasurementMetadata metadata,
            final String bearerToken) {

        // >>> CHANGE
        final var request = OpenAiPayloadBuilder.buildRequest(systemPrompt);
        // <<< CHANGE

        if (log.isInfoEnabled()) {
            log.info("Sending prompt to OpenAI Vision:\n{}", systemPrompt);
        }
        return webClientBuilder
                .build()
                .post()
                .uri(OPENAI_URL)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + openAiApiKey)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(request)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(OpenAiPayloadBuilder.OpenAiResponse.class)
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(15))
                .retryWhen(Retry.backoff(3, Duration.ofSeconds(3)).filter(this::isTransientError))
                .flatMap(this::parseResponse);
    }

    private boolean isTransientError(final Throwable throwable) {
        if (throwable instanceof WebClientResponseException wcre) {
            return wcre.getStatusCode().is5xxServerError() || wcre.getStatusCode().value() == 429;
        }
        return throwable instanceof java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;
    }

    private Mono<BodyMeasurementResult> parseResponse(
            final OpenAiPayloadBuilder.OpenAiResponse response) {

        return Mono.fromCallable(
                        () -> {
                            if (response == null || response.choices() == null || response.choices().isEmpty()) {
                                throw new IllegalStateException("Empty response from OpenAI");
                            }
                            final String jsonContent = response.choices().get(0).message().content();
                            return objectMapper.readValue(jsonContent, BodyMeasurementResult.class);
                        })
                .onErrorMap(e -> new RuntimeException("Failed to parse OpenAI Vision output", e));
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/NeuroIaMeasurementAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClientResponseException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.UserMeasurementMetadata;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.AiMeasurementPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.BodyMeasurementResult;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.common.ReqBase;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.common.RespBase;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
import reactor.util.retry.Retry;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

@Slf4j
@Component
@Primary
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class NeuroIaMeasurementAdapter implements AiMeasurementPort {

    private final WebClient.Builder webClientBuilder;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    @Value("${neuro-ia.api.url}")
    private String neuroIaUrl;

    public Mono<BodyMeasurementResult> extractMeasurements(
            final String systemPrompt,
            final UserMeasurementMetadata metadata,
            final String bearerToken) {
        log.info("[NeuroIA-Adapter] Requesting anthropometric analysis to centralized NeuroIA MS...");

        final ReqBase<Map<String, Object>> request = new ReqBase<>();
        request.setPayload(
                Map.of(
                        "systemPrompt", systemPrompt,
                        "userMetadata", metadata));

        return webClientBuilder
                .build()
                .post()
                .uri(neuroIaUrl + "/private/v1/ia/iaService/extract-measurements")
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearerToken)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(request)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(String.class)
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(20))
                // >>> CHANGE: Añadido log para observabilidad del raw payload antes de ser procesado
                .doOnNext(raw -> log.info("[NeuroIA-Adapter] Received RAW response: {}", raw))
                // <<< CHANGE
                .retryWhen(Retry.backoff(2, Duration.ofSeconds(3)).filter(this::isTransientError))
                .flatMap(this::parseNeuroIaResponse);
    }

    private boolean isTransientError(final Throwable throwable) {
        if (throwable instanceof WebClientResponseException wcre) {
            return wcre.getStatusCode().is5xxServerError() || wcre.getStatusCode().value() == 429;
        }
        return throwable instanceof java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;
    }

    private Mono<BodyMeasurementResult> parseNeuroIaResponse(final String rawResponse) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(
                        () -> {
                            final RespBase<BodyMeasurementResult> response =
                                    objectMapper.readValue(
                                            rawResponse, new TypeReference<RespBase<BodyMeasurementResult>>() {
                                            });

                            if (response != null && response.getPayload() != null) {
                                // Aceptar el payload si no hay un status de error EXPLÍCITO.
                                if (response.getStatus() == null || !Boolean.FALSE.equals(response.getStatus().getSuccess())) {
                                    return response.getPayload();
                                }
                            }

                            final String errorMsg =
                                    (response != null
                                            && response.getStatus() != null
                                            && response.getStatus().getError() != null
                                            && response.getStatus().getError().getMessages() != null)
                                            ? String.join(", ", response.getStatus().getError().getMessages())
                                            : "Invalid RespBase structure. Raw: " + rawResponse;

                            throw new BusinessLogicException("NeuroIA MS rejected the request: " + errorMsg);
                        })
                .onErrorMap(e -> {
                    if (e instanceof BusinessLogicException) {
                        return e;
                    }
                    return new RuntimeException("Failed to map response from NeuroIA MS", e);
                });
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/persistence/measurement/CustomerBiometricProfileMongoAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.persistence.measurement;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.persistence.CustomerBiometricProfilePort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.CustomerBiometricProfile;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomerBiometricProfileMongoAdapter implements CustomerBiometricProfilePort {

    private final CustomerBiometricProfileRepository repository;

    @Override
    public Mono<CustomerBiometricProfile> saveOrUpdate(CustomerBiometricProfile profile) {
        return repository.findByAccountIdAndTenantId(profile.accountId(), profile.tenantId())
                .flatMap(existing -> {
                    CustomerBiometricProfileEntity updated = new CustomerBiometricProfileEntity(
                            existing.id(),
                            existing.accountId(),
                            profile.height() != null ? profile.height() : existing.height(),
                            profile.weight() != null ? profile.weight() : existing.weight(),
                            profile.fitPreference() != null ? profile.fitPreference() : existing.fitPreference(),
                            profile.chestCm() != null ? profile.chestCm() : existing.chestCm(),
                            profile.shoulderCm() != null ? profile.shoulderCm() : existing.shoulderCm(),
                            Instant.now(),
                            existing.tenantId()
                    );
                    return repository.save(updated);
                })
                .switchIfEmpty(Mono.defer(() -> {
                    CustomerBiometricProfileEntity newEntity = new CustomerBiometricProfileEntity(
                            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                            profile.accountId(),
                            profile.height(),
                            profile.weight(),
                            profile.fitPreference(),
                            profile.chestCm(),
                            profile.shoulderCm(),
                            Instant.now(),
                            profile.tenantId()
                    );
                    return repository.save(newEntity);
                }))
                .map(saved -> new CustomerBiometricProfile(
                        saved.id(),
                        saved.accountId(),
                        saved.height(),
                        saved.weight(),
                        saved.fitPreference(),
                        saved.chestCm(),
                        saved.shoulderCm(),
                        saved.lastUpdated(),
                        saved.tenantId()
                ));
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/ProductRestAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;


import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.core.ParameterizedTypeReference;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.ProductExternalPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.Garment;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.measurement.ProductMeasurementModel;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.common.RespBase;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Enterprise WebClient adapter to communicate with the Support Microservice. Follows Port & Adapters
 * pattern by implementing ProductExternalPort to fetch garments.
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class ProductRestAdapter implements ProductExternalPort {

    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final String supportServiceUrl;

    public ProductRestAdapter(
            final WebClient.Builder webClientBuilder,
            @Value("${external-api.support-ms.url}") final String supportServiceUrl) {
        this.webClient = webClientBuilder.build();
        this.supportServiceUrl = supportServiceUrl;
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<Garment> getProductById(final String productId, final String bearerToken) {
        return webClient
                .get()
                .uri(supportServiceUrl + "/private/v1/support/product/" + productId)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearerToken)
                .retrieve()
                .onStatus(
                        status -> status.value() == 404,
                        response ->
                                Mono.error(
                                        new ResourceNotFoundException("Product not found in Support MS: " + productId)))
                .onStatus(
                        status -> status.is4xxClientError() || status.is5xxServerError(),
                        response ->
                                response
                                        .bodyToMono(String.class)
                                        .flatMap(
                                                body -> Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException("Support API error: " + body))))
                .bodyToMono(new ParameterizedTypeReference<RespBase<Map<String, Object>>>() {
                })
                .flatMap(
                        response -> {
                            if (response != null
                                    && Boolean.TRUE.equals(response.getStatus().getSuccess())
                                    && response.getPayload() != null) {
                                return Mono.just(mapToGarment(response.getPayload()));
                            }
                            return Mono.error(
                                    new BusinessLogicException("Invalid response for product: " + productId));
                        })
                .doOnSuccess(
                        g -> log.info("Successfully retrieved garment {} from Support MS", productId))
                .doOnError(e -> log.error("Failed to retrieve garment {}: {}", productId, e.getMessage()));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Garment mapToGarment(final Map<String, Object> payload) {
        final String id = payload.containsKey("id") ? String.valueOf(payload.get("id")) : "UNKNOWN";
        final String category =
                payload.containsKey("category") ? String.valueOf(payload.get("category")) : "Desconocida";

        List<ProductMeasurementModel> measurements = Collections.emptyList();

        if (payload.containsKey("measurements") && payload.get("measurements") instanceof List) {
            final List<Map<String, Object>> measList =
                    (List<Map<String, Object>>) payload.get("measurements");

            measurements =
                    measList.stream()
                            .map(
                                    m ->
                                            new ProductMeasurementModel(
                                                    m.containsKey("size") ? String.valueOf(m.get("size")) : "N/A",
                                                    parseDouble(m.get("chestContour")),
                                                    parseDouble(m.get("shoulderWidth")),
                                                    parseDouble(m.get("totalLength")),
                                                    parseDouble(m.get("sleeveLength"))))
                            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        }

        return new Garment(id, category, measurements);
    }

    private Double parseDouble(final Object value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return Double.parseDouble(String.valueOf(value));
        } catch (final NumberFormatException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

# 🤖 Agent: Enterprise Java WebFlux Architect & AI Engineer

**Rol:** Eres un **Senior Java Architect & Backend AI Integration Expert** con más de 15 años de experiencia. Tu objetivo es entregar código de producción 100% robusto, asíncrono y mantenible.

## 🛠️ Stack Tecnológico Obligatorio
- **Java 25** (Modo Preview activado, uso intensivo de Records, Pattern Matching, Switch Expressions).
- **Spring Boot 3.x (Puro WebFlux)** - *ESTRICTAMENTE PROHIBIDO* usar dependencias Servlet o Tomcat. Todo debe ser asíncrono (`Mono`, `Flux`).
- **Arquitectura Hexagonal (Ports & Adapters)**.
- **MongoDB Reactive** (`ReactiveMongoRepository`).
- **Apache PDFBox 3.0.3** (Para el motor de procesamiento PDF).
- WebClient para llamadas a APIs externas (NeuroIA, Evolution API, Support MS).

## 🏗️ Reglas de Arquitectura (Hexagonal Architecture)
Respeta estrictamente las capas, aislando el dominio de la infraestructura:
1. `@domain`: Entidades puras, excepciones de negocio, records (`PdfDocument`, `WhatsAppMessage`). Cero dependencias de Spring aquí.
2. `@application`: Casos de uso (`*UseCase`), servicios implementados (`*Service`), puertos de entrada (`*UseCase`) y puertos de salida (`*Port`).
3. `@infrastructure`: Adaptadores de entrada (RestControllers, WebHooks, WebSockets) y adaptadores de salida (MongoAdapters, WebClientAdapters).

## 🚫 Código (CERO TOLERANCIA)
- **NUNCA** uses `RestTemplate`, `FeignClient` o llamadas bloqueantes. Solo `WebClient`.
- **NUNCA** uses `.block()` en cadenas reactivas. Si necesitas extraer datos, usa `.map()`, `.flatMap()`, `.doOnNext()`, o `.deferContextual()`.
- **NUNCA** uses clases anémicas si puedes usar `records` inmutables (DTOs y Modelos deben ser records siempre que sea posible).
- **CERO** `NullPointerException`. Utiliza flujos reactivos seguros, `.defaultIfEmpty()` o `.switchIfEmpty()`.
- No añadas librerías a menos que sea estrictamente necesario.

## 🗺️ Patrones de Rutas Estrictos (¡NO INVENTAR, USAR ESTAS!)
Cuando se te pida crear o modificar un archivo, DEBES usar estas rutas:
- **Modelos/Records:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/domain/model/<nombre>.java`
- **Puertos Entrada:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/port/inbound/<nombre>UseCase.java`
- **Puertos Salida:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/port/outbound/<nombre>Port.java`
- **Servicios:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/service/<nombre>Service.java`
- **Controladores:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/inbound/rest/<nombre>Controller.java`
- **Adaptadores BD/API:** `src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/<nombre>Adapter.java`

## 🎯 Restricciones de Salida (Output Constraint)
**DEPENDE DE LA SOLICITUD DEL USUARIO:**
- Si pide código: Devuelve **SOLO CÓDIGO FINAL** listo para compilar. Sin explicaciones innecesarias, sin placeholders estilo `// TODO: implement logic`.
- Usa Lombok (`@Slf4j`, `@RequiredArgsConstructor`, `@Builder`) para reducir el código boilerplate.
- La respuesta final de los controladores DEBE estar envuelta en la clase `RespBase<T>`.

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/document/application/service/PdfGenerationService.java

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.inbound.PdfGenerationUseCase;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.outbound.PdfRendererPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.domain.model.PdfGenerationRequest;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * Orquestador trivial: recibe un HTML completo y lo delega al renderer.
 * Sin fetch de plantillas, sin Thymeleaf, sin conocimiento de tenant.
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PdfGenerationService implements PdfGenerationUseCase {

    private final PdfRendererPort pdfRendererPort;

    @Override
    public Mono<byte[]> generatePdf(
            final PdfGenerationRequest request,
            final String bearerToken,
            final String tenantId) {

        return Mono.justOrEmpty(request)
                .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(
                        new BusinessLogicException("PdfGenerationRequest es requerido")))
                .filter(req -> req.html() != null && !req.html().isBlank())
                .switchIfEmpty(Mono.error(
                        new BusinessLogicException("El campo 'html' es obligatorio y no puede estar vacío")))
                .flatMap(req -> pdfRendererPort.render(req.html()))
                .doOnSuccess(bytes -> log.info(
                        "PDF generado correctamente: {} bytes (tenant={})",
                        bytes.length, tenantId))
                .doOnError(err -> log.error(
                        "Error generando PDF (tenant={}): {}",
                        tenantId, err.getMessage()));
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/document/application/service/PdfFileNameBuilder.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.service;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Construye el nombre del archivo PDF usando varios parámetros (código de
 * plantilla, número de cotización) y la fecha/hora de generación.
 */
@Component
public class PdfFileNameBuilder {

    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIMESTAMP_FORMAT = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss");
    private static final String FALLBACK_CODE = "document";

    public String buildFileName(final String templateCode, final Map<String, Object> variables) {
        final String code = sanitize(templateCode == null || templateCode.isBlank() ? FALLBACK_CODE : templateCode);
        final String quotation = extractQuotationNumber(variables);
        final String timestamp = LocalDateTime.now().format(TIMESTAMP_FORMAT);

        final String base = quotation.isEmpty()
                ? code + "_" + timestamp
                : code + "_" + quotation + "_" + timestamp;

        return base + ".pdf";
    }

    private String extractQuotationNumber(final Map<String, Object> variables) {
        if (variables == null || variables.isEmpty()) {
            return "";
        }
        final Object value = variables.get("quotationNumber");
        return value == null ? "" : sanitize(String.valueOf(value));
    }

    private String sanitize(final String raw) {
        if (raw == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return raw.replaceAll("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|\\s]+", "_").trim();
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.infrastructure.config;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary;
import org.thymeleaf.TemplateEngine;
import org.thymeleaf.templatemode.TemplateMode;
import org.thymeleaf.templateresolver.StringTemplateResolver;

/**
 * Configura un motor Thymeleaf en memoria con StringTemplateResolver, porque los
 * HTML provienen de support-ms (BD), no del classpath /resources/templates.
 */
@Configuration
public class ThymeleafConfig {
    @Bean
    @Primary
    public TemplateEngine documentTemplateEngine() {
        final StringTemplateResolver resolver = new StringTemplateResolver();
        resolver.setTemplateMode(TemplateMode.HTML);
        resolver.setCacheable(false);

        final TemplateEngine engine = new TemplateEngine();
        engine.setTemplateResolver(resolver);
        return engine;
    }
}

            @Value("${application.pdf-document.base-uri:https://alamodaperu.online/}") final String baseUri) {

        return new PdfRendererSettings(resolved);
    }

    }

===================================================
FILE: .dsh/java-webflux-feature/skills/SKILL.md
===================================================

---
name: java-webflux-hexagonal-feature
description: Flujo de trabajo estandarizado para crear, modificar o auditar endpoints, casos de uso, adaptadores y modelos en el backend Java WebFlux.
whenToUse: Cuando el usuario solicite crear un nuevo endpoint REST, integrar una API externa (WebClient), refactorizar lógica de negocio o trabajar en persistencia reactiva.
---

# Flujo de Trabajo para Desarrollo Backend (Java WebFlux + Arquitectura Hexagonal)

## 1. Análisis de Impacto (Fase de Arquitectura)
Antes de escribir código, analiza:
- **Flujo Reactivo:** ¿La operación es I/O bound? Toda la cadena debe retornar `Mono` o `Flux`.
- **Arquitectura Hexagonal:** ¿Qué capa se ve afectada? Respeta la regla de dependencia: Infraestructura depende de Aplicación, Aplicación depende de Dominio. El Dominio no depende de NADA.
- **Manejo de Errores:** Define dónde y cómo capturar la excepción. Todas las respuestas HTTP de error deben mapearse al formato estándar `RespBase<T>` usando el `ReactiveGlobalExceptionHandler`.

## 2. Implementación de Código (Reglas Estrictas)
- **Dominio (`@domain`):** 
  - Usa `record` para todos los modelos inmutables de datos.
  - Las excepciones de negocio extienden de `BusinessLogicException`.
- **Aplicación (`@application`):**
  - **Puertos de Entrada:** Interfaces terminadas en `*UseCase`.
  - **Puertos de Salida:** Interfaces terminadas en `*Port` (ej: `*PersistencePort`, `*ExternalPort`).
  - **Servicios:** Implementan los UseCases. Usa `@Service`, `@RequiredArgsConstructor`, e inyecta los `Port` como variables `final`.
- **Infraestructura (`@infrastructure`):**
  - **Controladores Rest:** Usa `@RestController`, inyecta el `UseCase`. Las respuestas siempre deben ir envueltas en `RespBase<T>`. Extrae el `TenantId` usando `TenantWebFilter.TENANT_KEY`.
  - **Adaptadores Salida:** Implementan los `Port`. Utiliza `WebClient` para APIs externas y `ReactiveMongoRepository` para persistencia.
- **Concurrencia y Hilos:** NUNCA uses `.block()`. Si debes usar una librería bloqueante legacy (ej. PDFBox), envuélvelo en `Mono.fromCallable(...)` y usa `.subscribeOn(Schedulers.boundedElastic())`.

## 3. Verificación de Calidad (Checklist del Arquitecto)
Antes de entregar el código, verifica mentalmente:
- [ ] ¿Hay llamadas bloqueantes encubiertas en la cadena reactiva?
- [ ] ¿Los DTOs y Entities son `records` (donde aplique)?
- [ ] ¿Se están gestionando los valores nulos con `.defaultIfEmpty()` o `.switchIfEmpty()` en lugar de simples condicionales `if (x == null)`?
- [ ] ¿El adaptador REST consume y produce los MediaTypes correctos (ej: `APPLICATION_JSON_VALUE`)?
- [ ] ¿Se usa `@Slf4j` para trazas de log importantes en lugar de `System.out`?
- [ ] ¿Se respetaron las rutas de los paquetes (dominio, aplicación, infraestructura)?

## 4. Formato de Salida Estricto
Aplica el formato de `CHANGE START/END` (si es requerido por tu IDE/Agent). Entrega código de producción final, limpio y sin comentarios residuales (cero `// TODO: implementar`). Usa sintaxis de Java 25 (Pattern matching, Text Blocks).

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.domain.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnoreProperties;

/**
 * Contrato de entrada para la generación de un PDF a partir de un HTML
 * completamente renderizado por el servicio consumidor (e.g. support-ms).

 * El Engine es STATELESS: no consulta plantillas, no conoce tenants, no
 * interpreta el negocio. Solo transforma el HTML recibido en bytes PDF.

 * @param html     HTML completo (DOCTYPE + head + style + body), ya
 *                 renderizado. Incluye el CSS embebido en {@code <style>}.

 *                 el Controller genera {@code document_yyyyMMdd_HHmmss.pdf}.
 */

public record PdfGenerationRequest(
        String html,
        String fileName

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/document/infrastructure/adapter/inbound/rest/PdfGenerationController.java

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.infrastructure.adapter.inbound.rest;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.inbound.PdfGenerationUseCase;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.domain.model.PdfGenerationRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.security.TenantWebFilter;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * Endpoint REST de generación de documentos PDF.
 * El cliente (support-ms) envía el HTML completo; el Engine solo lo renderiza.
 *
 * Cubierto por:
 *  - /private/v1/pdf/** (JWT) en SecurityConfig
 *  - TenantWebFilter (X-Tenant-ID obligatorio en /private/**)
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/private/v1/pdf/generate")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PdfGenerationController {

    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIMESTAMP_FORMAT =
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss");
    private static final String FALLBACK_FILE_NAME_PREFIX = "document_";

    private final PdfGenerationUseCase pdfGenerationUseCase;

    @PostMapping(value = "/document", produces = MediaType.APPLICATION_PDF_VALUE)
    public Mono<ResponseEntity<byte[]>> generateDocument(
            @RequestBody final PdfGenerationRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION) final String bearerToken) {

        return Mono.deferContextual(ctx -> {
            final String tenantId = ctx.getOrDefault(TenantWebFilter.TENANT_KEY, "DEFAULT");

            return pdfGenerationUseCase.generatePdf(request, bearerToken, tenantId)
                    .map(bytes -> {
                        final String fileName = resolveFileName(request);
                        log.info("Documento generado: {} ({} bytes, tenant={})",
                                fileName, bytes.length, tenantId);

                        return ResponseEntity.ok()
                                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_PDF)
                                .header(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                                        "attachment; filename=\"" + fileName + "\"")
                                .body(bytes);
                    });
        });
    }

    private String resolveFileName(final PdfGenerationRequest request) {
        if (request != null && request.fileName() != null && !request.fileName().isBlank()) {
            return sanitizeFileName(request.fileName());
        }
        return FALLBACK_FILE_NAME_PREFIX
                + LocalDateTime.now().format(TIMESTAMP_FORMAT)
                + ".pdf";
    }

    private String sanitizeFileName(final String raw) {
        final String cleaned = raw.replaceAll("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|\\s]+", "_").trim();
        return cleaned.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf") ? cleaned : cleaned + ".pdf";
    }
}

 * Extiende {@link BusinessLogicException} para ser manejada por el
 * {@code ReactiveGlobalExceptionHandler} existente (HTTP 422).
 */
public class PdfGenerationException extends BusinessLogicException {

    public PdfGenerationException(final String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public PdfGenerationException(final String message, final Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/document/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/TemplateWebClientAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.core.ParameterizedTypeReference;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.outbound.TemplateProviderPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.domain.model.TemplateDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.common.RespBase;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * Adaptador de salida que obtiene plantillas HTML desde support-ms vía WebClient.
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class TemplateWebClientAdapter implements TemplateProviderPort {

    private static final String TEMPLATE_PATH = "/private/v1/support/templates/{code}";

    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final String supportServiceUrl;

    public TemplateWebClientAdapter(
            final WebClient.Builder webClientBuilder,
            @Value("${external-api.support-ms.url}") final String supportServiceUrl) {
        this.webClient = webClientBuilder.build();
        this.supportServiceUrl = supportServiceUrl;
    }
    @Override
    public Mono<TemplateDocument> fetchTemplate(final String templateCode,
                                                final String bearerToken,
                                                final String tenantId) {
        return webClient.get()
                .uri(supportServiceUrl + TEMPLATE_PATH, templateCode)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, bearerToken)
                .header("X-Tenant-ID", tenantId)
                .retrieve()
                .onStatus(status -> status.value() == 404,
                        response -> Mono.error(new ResourceNotFoundException(
                                "Plantilla no encontrada: " + templateCode)))
                .onStatus(status -> status.is4xxClientError() || status.is5xxServerError(),
                        response -> response.bodyToMono(String.class)
                                .flatMap(body -> Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException(
                                        "Error en support-ms: " + body))))
                .bodyToMono(new ParameterizedTypeReference<RespBase<TemplateDocument>>() {
                })
                .flatMap(response -> {
                    if (response != null
                            && Boolean.TRUE.equals(response.getStatus().getSuccess())
                            && response.getPayload() != null) {
                        return Mono.just(response.getPayload());
                    }
                    final String errorMsg = (response != null && response.getStatus().getError() != null)
                            ? String.join(", ", response.getStatus().getError().getMessages())
                            : "Error desconocido";
                    return Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException(
                            "Error obteniendo plantilla desde support-ms: " + errorMsg));
                })
                .doOnSuccess(t -> log.info("Plantilla '{}' obtenida desde support-ms", templateCode))
                .doOnError(e -> log.error("Error obteniendo plantilla '{}': {}", templateCode, e.getMessage()));
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/document/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/HtmlToPdfAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import com.openhtmltopdf.pdfboxout.PdfRendererBuilder;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.outbound.PdfRendererPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.domain.exception.PdfGenerationException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.infrastructure.config.PdfRendererConfig;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
import reactor.core.scheduler.Schedulers;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Adaptador de salida que convierte HTML a PDF con OpenHTMLtoPDF (PDFBox 3).
 * Operación síncrona aislada en boundedElastic para no bloquear el Event Loop.
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class HtmlToPdfAdapter implements PdfRendererPort {

    private final String baseUri;

    public HtmlToPdfAdapter(final PdfRendererConfig.PdfRendererSettings settings) {
        this.baseUri = settings.baseUri();
    }
    @Override
    public Mono<byte[]> render(final String processedHtml) {

        if (processedHtml == null || processedHtml.isBlank()) {
            return Mono.error(new PdfGenerationException("HTML content is null or empty"));
        }

        String safeHtml = sanitizeHtmlForXmlParser(processedHtml);

        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
                    final ByteArrayOutputStream outputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    try {
                        final PdfRendererBuilder builder = new PdfRendererBuilder();
                        builder.useFastMode();
                        builder.withHtmlContent(safeHtml, null);
                        builder.toStream(outputStream);
                        builder.run();
                        return outputStream.toByteArray();
                    } finally {
                        try {
                            outputStream.close();
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {
                        }
                    }
                })
                .onErrorMap(ex -> {
                    if (ex instanceof PdfGenerationException) {
                        return ex;
                    }
                    String errorMsg = ex.getMessage() != null ? ex.getMessage() : ex.getClass().getSimpleName();
                    if (ex.getClass().getName().contains("XRRuntimeException") || errorMsg.contains("SAXParseException")) {
                        return new PdfGenerationException(
                                "Malformed XHTML. OpenHTMLtoPDF requires strict XML. Check for unclosed tags or unescaped '&'. Details: "
                                        + errorMsg,
                                ex);
                    }
                    return new PdfGenerationException("Error rendering HTML to PDF: " + errorMsg, ex);
                })
                .subscribeOn(Schedulers.boundedElastic());
    }

    private String sanitizeHtmlForXmlParser(final String rawHtml) {
        String cleaned = rawHtml;

        // 1. Arreglar el error del payload: <style>{ -> <style>body {
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("(?i)<style>\\s*\\{", "<style>body {");

        // 2. Fix invalid CSS in the client payload: @page ( ... ) -> @page { ... }
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("@page\\s*\\(([^)]*)\\)", "@page {$1}");
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("(?i)min-height\\s*:\\s*auto", "min-height: 100%");
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("(?i)<br\\s*>", "<br/>");
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("(?i)<hr\\s*>", "<hr/>");
        cleaned = cleaned.replaceAll("(?i)<img([^>]*?)(?<!/)>", "<img$1/>");

        // 5. Extraer todos los <style> del body
        StringBuilder styles = new StringBuilder();
        Pattern stylePattern = Pattern.compile("(?is)<style[^>]*>.*?</style>");
        Matcher matcher = stylePattern.matcher(cleaned);
        while (matcher.find()) {
            styles.append(matcher.group());
        }

        // Remover los <style> del body para que no se impriman como texto
        cleaned = matcher.replaceAll("");

        // 6. Empaquetar todo en un documento XHTML estricto (estilos en el head)
        String lower = cleaned.toLowerCase().trim();
        if (!lower.startsWith("<!doctype") && !lower.startsWith("<html")) {
            return "<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset=\"UTF-8\"/>"
                    + styles.toString()
                    + "</head><body>"
                    + cleaned
                    + "</body></html>";
        }

        // Si ya viene con <html>, inyectamos los estilos al inicio
        return cleaned.replaceFirst("(?i)<head>", "<head>" + styles.toString());
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model;

/**
 * Tipos de elemento del editor PDF.
 * Se usan como contrato estable entre la conversión determinística, el frontend y la IA.
 */
public enum PdfEditorElementType {
    /** Texto editable (palabra o línea). */
    TEXT,
    /** Imagen incrustada (data URI en {@code src}). */
    IMAGE,
    /** Rectángulo / línea vectorial. */
    RECT,
    /** Tabla reconstruida (hijos = celdas). */
    TABLE,
    /** Celda de una tabla. */
    TABLE_CELL
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnoreProperties;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;

import java.io.Serializable;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Elemento editable dentro de una página. Todas las coordenadas y medidas están en puntos (pt).
 * La posición (x, y) es la esquina superior izquierda (origen CSS).
 */
@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record PdfEditorElement(
        String id,
        PdfEditorElementType type,
        float x,
        float y,
        float width,
        float height,
        /** Contenido de texto (para TEXT y TABLE_CELL). */
        String text,
        /** Propiedades CSS clave (fontFamily, fontSize, color, fontWeight, fontStyle, ...). */

        /** data URI para IMAGE. */
        String src,
        /** Hijos: celdas de una TABLE, o elementos anidados. */
        List<PdfEditorElement> children,
        /** Tipo semántico asignado por la IA (ej. heading, amount, table-header). Opcional. */

) implements Serializable {

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/service/PdfEditorService.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.service;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorExportRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorRefineRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.inbound.PdfEditorUseCase;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfProcessingPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfToHtmlPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfVisionPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.document.application.port.outbound.PdfRendererPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Orquestador del editor PDF.
 *
 * <ul>
 *   <li><b>importar</b>: PDF → HTML editable (determinístico) → enriquecimiento con IA (tolerante a fallos).</li>
 *   <li><b>exportar</b>: HTML por página → PDF por página (openhtmltopdf) → merge (PDFBox).</li>
 *   <li><b>refinar</b>: mejora de fidelidad del documento editable vía IA.</li>
 * </ul>
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PdfEditorService implements PdfEditorUseCase {

    private final PdfToHtmlPort pdfToHtmlPort;
    private final PdfVisionPort pdfVisionPort;
    private final PdfRendererPort pdfRendererPort;
    private final PdfProcessingPort pdfProcessingPort;

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> importPdf(final byte[] pdfBytes, final String bearerToken) {
        if (pdfBytes == null || pdfBytes.length == 0) {
            return Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException("El PDF proporcionado está vacío."));
        }

        return pdfToHtmlPort.toHtml(pdfBytes)
                .flatMap(doc -> pdfVisionPort.enhance(doc, bearerToken)
                        .onErrorResume(e -> {
                            log.warn("IA no disponible; se devuelve el documento determinístico: {}",
                                    e.getMessage());
                            return Mono.just(doc);
                        }))
                .doOnSuccess(doc -> log.info("PDF importado al editor: {} páginas (documentId={})",
                        doc.pages() == null ? 0 : doc.pages().size(), doc.documentId()));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<byte[]> exportPdf(final PdfEditorExportRequest request) {
        if (request == null || request.pageHtmls() == null || request.pageHtmls().isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException("No se proporcionaron páginas para exportar."));
        }

        return Flux.fromIterable(request.pageHtmls())
                .flatMapSequential(html -> pdfRendererPort.render(html)
                        .map(bytes -> new PdfDocument(
                                "page.pdf", bytes, bytes.length, null, Map.of(), List.of())))
                .collectList()
                .flatMap(pdfProcessingPort::merge)
                .map(PdfDocument::content)
                .doOnSuccess(bytes -> log.info("PDF exportado desde el editor: {} bytes", bytes.length))
                .doOnError(e -> log.error("Error exportando PDF desde el editor: {}", e.getMessage(), e));
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(final PdfEditorRefineRequest request, final String bearerToken) {
        if (request == null || request.document() == null) {
            return Mono.error(new BusinessLogicException("Se requiere el documento para refinar."));
        }
        return pdfVisionPort.refine(request.document(), request.screenshots(), bearerToken);
    }
}


===================================================
FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/dto/PdfEditorRefineRequest.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto;

 * @param screenshots capturas (data URI base64) de cada página, para comparación visual
 */

        @JsonProperty("document") PdfEditorDocument document,
        @JsonProperty("screenshots") List<String> screenshots

FILE: docs/pdf-editor.md
===================================================

# Editor de PDF — Contrato y arquitectura

Pipeline **PDF → HTML editable → PDF** con asistencia de IA, en el backend `pillihuaman-com-pe-engine`.

## Flujo

```
PDF ──► /editor/import ──► PDFBox extrae (texto+color+imágenes) ──► IA reconstruye tablas
                                    │
                                    ▼
                        PdfEditorDocument (HTML + elementos)
                                    │
                        frontend WYSIWYG edita (HTML/elementos)
                                    │
                                    ▼
                        /editor/export ──► openhtmltopdf por página ──► PDFBox merge ──► PDF final
```

## Endpoints

Base: `/private/v1/pdf/editor` (requiere JWT `Authorization`; cubierto por `/private/v1/pdf/**`).

### 1. Importar — `POST /editor/import` (multipart)
- `file`: PDF (`multipart/form-data`).
- Respuesta: `RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>` (200).

### 2. Exportar — `POST /editor/export` (JSON → PDF)
```json
{ "fileName": "factura.pdf", "pages": ["<!DOCTYPE html>…", "…"] }
```
- Cada `pages[i]` es el HTML completo (ya editado) de una página, con su `@page size`.
- Respuesta: PDF descargable (`application/pdf`).

### 3. Refinar — `POST /editor/refine` (JSON)
```json
{ "document": { "…": "PdfEditorDocument" }, "screenshots": ["data:image/png;base64,…"] }
```
- `screenshots[i]` = captura de la página `i` (opcional). La IA (OpenAI visión) corrige
  posiciones/tamaños/fuentes para igualar la captura.
- Respuesta: `RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>` refinado.

## Modelo `PdfEditorDocument`

```json
{
  "documentId": "doc_1699999999999",
  "metadata": null,
  "pages": [
    {
      "pageNumber": 1,
      "widthPt": 595,
      "heightPt": 842,
      "html": "<!DOCTYPE html>…",
      "elements": [
        {
          "id": "uuid",
          "type": "TEXT",          // TEXT | IMAGE | RECT | TABLE | TABLE_CELL
          "x": 72, "y": 100,       // esquina superior izquierda, en pt
          "width": 120, "height": 14,
          "text": "FACTURA",
          "style": { "fontFamily": "Helvetica", "fontSize": "12", "color": "#000000",
                     "fontWeight": "bold", "fontStyle": "normal" },
          "src": null,              // data URI en IMAGE
          "children": []            // celdas en TABLE
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

Unidades: **puntos (pt)** en todo el contrato (coincide con PDFBox y openhtmltopdf, sin conversión).

## Configuración

```properties
application.pdf.editor.ai-provider=openai   # openai | neuroia | none
application.pdf.editor.openai.model=gpt-4o
openai.api.key=sk-...
```

## Proveedores de IA (puerto `PdfVisionPort`)

| Provider | `enhance` (importar) | `refine` (fidelidad) |
|----------|----------------------|----------------------|
| `openai` | Tablas + OCR de escaneados (gpt-4o visión, JSON) | Corrige posiciones vs. screenshot (visión) |
| `neuroia` | Clasifica semánticamente los textos (`data-semantic`) | bridge pendiente (`PdfEditableStructure`) |
| `none`   | sin cambios | sin cambios |

Tolerancia a fallos: si la IA falla o no está configurada, el flujo devuelve el documento
determinístico sin cambios (nunca bloquea la importación/exportación).

## Estructura de clases (hexagonal)

```
domain/model            PdfEditorDocument, PdfEditorPage, PdfEditorElement, PdfEditorElementType
application/port/inbound  PdfEditorUseCase
application/port/outbound PdfToHtmlPort, PdfVisionPort
application/service     PdfEditorService
application/dto         PdfEditorExportRequest, PdfEditorRefineRequest
infrastructure/adapter/inbound/rest   PdfEditorController
infrastructure/adapter/outbound       PdfBoxToHtmlAdapter, PdfEditorHtmlBuilder
infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external  OpenAiPdfVisionAdapter, NeuroIaPdfVisionAdapter,
                                          NoOpPdfVisionAdapter, PdfVisionRouter
```

## Pendiente

1. Puente NeuroIA `refine` (comparación visual sobre `PdfEditableStructure`).
2. Verificación de compilación (ver más abajo).
3. Frontend WYSIWYG (CKEditor/TinyMCE/GrapesJS) que consuma este contrato.

## Compilar

```powershell
$env:JAVA_HOME = "C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-25"
mvn clean compile
```

Requiere JDK 25 y acceso al repositorio Maven (el parent `spring-boot-starter-parent:4.0.0-M1`
es milestone; configurar `repo.spring.io/milestone` si no está disponible en el mirror).


===================================================
FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/application/port/outbound/PdfVisionPort.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound;

import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Puerto de salida: mejora del documento editable mediante IA (tablas, orden de lectura,
 * reconstrucción de bloques semánticos y refinamiento de fidelidad).
 *
 * <p>Implementaciones: OpenAI gpt-4o, NeuroIA (servicio interno) o NoOp.
 * La selección se realiza tras un único bean {@code @Primary} configurado por
 * {@code application.pdf.editor.ai-provider}.</p>
 */
public interface PdfVisionPort {

    /**
     * Enriquece el documento determinístico con estructura semántica obtenida por IA.
     * Debe ser tolerante a fallos: si la IA no está disponible, retorna el documento sin cambios.
     */
    Mono<PdfEditorDocument> enhance(PdfEditorDocument document, String bearerToken);

    /**
     * Refina la fidelidad del documento editable comparando cada página con su captura
     * (screenshot). Debe ser tolerante a fallos: si no hay capturas o la IA falla,
     * retorna el documento sin cambios.
     */
    Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(PdfEditorDocument document, List<String> screenshots, String bearerToken);
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/PdfBoxToHtmlAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.pdfbox.Loader;
import org.apache.pdfbox.contentstream.PDFGraphicsStreamEngine;
import org.apache.pdfbox.cos.COSName;
import org.apache.pdfbox.io.RandomAccessReadBuffer;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDPage;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.common.PDRectangle;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.graphics.color.PDColor;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.graphics.form.PDFormXObject;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.graphics.image.PDImage;
import org.apache.pdfbox.text.PDFTextStripper;
import org.apache.pdfbox.text.TextPosition;
import org.apache.pdfbox.util.Matrix;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfToHtmlPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.exception.BusinessLogicException;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElement;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElementType;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorPage;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;
import reactor.core.scheduler.Schedulers;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.geom.Point2D;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.Writer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Conversor determinístico PDF → HTML editable usando Apache PDFBox 3.
 *
 * <p>Genera, por cada página, un HTML con posicionamiento absoluto (fiel al layout original),
 * preservando texto (palabra a palabra con fuente, tamaño, color y estilo) e imágenes.
 * Todas las unidades se expresan en puntos (pt), coincidiendo con el espacio de PDFBox y de
 * openhtmltopdf, lo que evita conversiones y pérdida de precisión.</p>
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class PdfBoxToHtmlAdapter implements PdfToHtmlPort {

    private static final float ASCENT_RATIO = 0.8f;   // aproximación de la altura de la ascendente
    private static final float LINE_HEIGHT_RATIO = 1.2f; // altura de línea aproximada

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> toHtml(final byte[] pdfBytes) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> convert(pdfBytes))
                .subscribeOn(Schedulers.boundedElastic());
    }

    private PdfEditorDocument convert(final byte[] pdfBytes) throws IOException {
        if (pdfBytes == null || pdfBytes.length == 0) {
            throw new BusinessLogicException("El PDF proporcionado está vacío.");
        }

        try (PDDocument document = Loader.loadPDF(new RandomAccessReadBuffer(pdfBytes))) {
            final List<PdfEditorPage> pages = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < document.getNumberOfPages(); i++) {
                pages.add(convertPage(document, i));
            }
            return new PdfEditorDocument("doc_" + System.currentTimeMillis(), pages, null);
        }
    }

    private PdfEditorPage convertPage(final PDDocument document, final int pageIndex) throws IOException {
        final PDPage page = document.getPage(pageIndex);
        final PDRectangle box = page.getMediaBox();
        final float width = box.getWidth();
        final float height = box.getHeight();

        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>();
        elements.addAll(extractTextElements(document, pageIndex, box));
        elements.addAll(extractImageElements(document, pageIndex, height));

        final String html = PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.buildPageHtml(pageIndex + 1, width, height, elements);
        return new PdfEditorPage(pageIndex + 1, width, height, html, elements);
    }

    // =====================================================================================
    // Extracción de texto (palabra a palabra, con fuente/tamaño/color)
    // =====================================================================================

    private List<PdfEditorElement> extractTextElements(
            final PDDocument document, final int pageIndex, final PDRectangle box) throws IOException {

        final float originX = box.getLowerLeftX();
        final float originY = box.getLowerLeftY();
        final float pageHeight = box.getHeight();

        final List<Word> words = new ArrayList<>();
        final IdentityHashMap<TextPosition, Integer> colorByPosition = new IdentityHashMap<>();

        final PDFTextStripper stripper = new PDFTextStripper() {
            @Override
            protected void processTextPosition(final TextPosition text) {
                super.processTextPosition(text);
                try {
                    final PDColor color = getGraphicsState().getNonStrokingColor();
                    colorByPosition.put(text, color == null ? null : color.toRGB());
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    colorByPosition.put(text, null);
                }
            }

            @Override
            protected void writeString(final String string, final List<TextPosition> textPositions) {
                if (string == null || string.trim().isEmpty()
                        || textPositions == null || textPositions.isEmpty()) {
                    return;
                }
                final TextPosition first = textPositions.get(0);
                final float x = first.getXDirAdj() - originX;
                final float baselineY = first.getYDirAdj() - originY;
                final float fontSize = first.getFontSizeInPt();

                float width = 0f;
                for (TextPosition tp : textPositions) {
                    width += tp.getWidthDirAdj();
                }

                final Integer rgb = colorByPosition.get(textPositions.get(textPositions.size() - 1));
                words.add(new Word(string.trim(), x, baselineY, width, fontSize,
                        first.getFont().getName(), rgb));
            }
        };

        stripper.setStartPage(pageIndex + 1);
        stripper.setEndPage(pageIndex + 1);
        stripper.writeText(document, Writer.nullWriter());

        // Orden de lectura: de arriba hacia abajo y, dentro de la misma línea, de izquierda a derecha.
        words.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(Word::baselineY).reversed()
                .thenComparingDouble(Word::x));

        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>(words.size());
        for (Word w : words) {
            final float top = pageHeight - w.baselineY() - (w.fontSize() * ASCENT_RATIO);
            final float height = w.fontSize() * LINE_HEIGHT_RATIO;
            elements.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                    UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                    PdfEditorElementType.TEXT,
                    w.x(), top, w.width(), height,
                    w.text(), buildTextStyle(w), null, null, null));
        }
        return elements;
    }

    private Map<String, String> buildTextStyle(final Word word) {
        final Map<String, String> style = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        style.put("fontFamily", toCssFont(word.fontName()));
        style.put("fontSize", PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.formatPt(word.fontSize()));
        style.put("color", toCssColor(word.color()));

        final String name = word.fontName() == null ? "" : word.fontName().toLowerCase();
        final boolean bold = name.contains("bold") || name.contains("black") || name.contains("heavy");
        final boolean italic = name.contains("italic") || name.contains("oblique");
        style.put("fontWeight", bold ? "bold" : "normal");
        style.put("fontStyle", italic ? "italic" : "normal");
        return style;
    }

    private String toCssFont(final String fontName) {
        if (fontName == null) {
            return "Helvetica";
        }
        final String name = fontName.toLowerCase();
        if (name.contains("courier") || name.contains("mono")) {
            return "Courier";
        }
        if (name.contains("times") || name.contains("serif")) {
            return "Times New Roman";
        }
        return "Helvetica";
    }

    private String toCssColor(final Integer rgb) {
        if (rgb == null) {
            return "#000000";
        }
        // rgb es el valor RGB empaquetado de PDColor.toRGB(); enmascaramos a 24 bits (RRGGBB).
        return String.format("#%06X", rgb & 0xFFFFFF);
    }

    // =====================================================================================
    // Extracción de imágenes (posiciones + data URI PNG)
    // =====================================================================================

    private List<PdfEditorElement> extractImageElements(
            final PDDocument document, final int pageIndex, final float pageHeight) throws IOException {

        final PDPage page = document.getPage(pageIndex);
        final List<PdfEditorElement> images = new ArrayList<>();

        final PDFGraphicsStreamEngine engine = new PDFGraphicsStreamEngine(page) {
            @Override
            public void drawImage(final PDImage pdImage) throws IOException {
                processImage(pdImage, getGraphicsState().getCurrentTransformationMatrix());
            }

            private void processImage(final PDImage pdImage, final Matrix ctm) {
                try {
                    final BufferedImage source = pdImage.getImage();
                    if (source == null) {
                        return;
                    }
                    // Normalizar a ARGB para asegurar que ImageIO pueda codificar a PNG.
                    final BufferedImage argb = new BufferedImage(
                            source.getWidth(), source.getHeight(), BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
                    final Graphics2D g = argb.createGraphics();
                    g.drawImage(source, 0, 0, null);
                    g.dispose();

                    final ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    if (!ImageIO.write(argb, "png", out)) {
                        return;
                    }
                    final String dataUri = "data:image/png;base64,"
                            + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(out.toByteArray());

                    final float width = ctm.getScalingFactorX();
                    final float height = ctm.getScalingFactorY();
                    final float x = ctm.getTranslateX();
                    final float yTop = pageHeight - (ctm.getTranslateY() + height);

                    images.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                            UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                            PdfEditorElementType.IMAGE,
                            x, yTop, width, height,
                            null, Map.of(), dataUri, null, null));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.warn("No se pudo extraer una imagen de la página {}: {}",
                            pageIndex + 1, e.getMessage());
                }
            }

            @Override
            public void showForm(final PDFormXObject form) throws IOException {
                final Iterable<COSName> xObjects = form.getResources().getXObjectNames();
                for (COSName name : xObjects) {
                    if (form.getResources().isImageXObject(name)) {
                        processImage((PDImage) form.getResources().getXObject(name),
                                getGraphicsState().getCurrentTransformationMatrix());
                    }
                }
            }

            // Operadores de gráficos no necesarios en esta fase (solo texto e imágenes).
            @Override public void appendRectangle(Point2D p0, Point2D p1, Point2D p2, Point2D p3) { }
            @Override public void moveTo(float x, float y) { }
            @Override public void lineTo(float x, float y) { }
            @Override public void curveTo(float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, float x3, float y3) { }
            @Override public Point2D getCurrentPoint() { return new Point2D.Float(0, 0); }
            @Override public void closePath() { }
            @Override public void endPath() { }
            @Override public void strokePath() { }
            @Override public void fillPath(int windingRule) { }
            @Override public void fillAndStrokePath(int windingRule) { }
            @Override public void shadingFill(COSName shadingName) { }
            @Override public void clip(int windingRule) { }
        };

        engine.processPage(page);
        return images;
    }

    /** Palabra extraída con su geometría y estilo. */
    private record Word(
            String text,
            float x,
            float baselineY,
            float width,
            float fontSize,
            String fontName,
            Integer color
    ) {
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnoreProperties;

/**
 * Documento PDF importado al editor: colección de páginas editables.
 */
@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)

        String documentId,

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.inbound;

import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorExportRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorRefineRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * Puerto de entrada del editor PDF: importar (PDF→HTML editable), exportar (HTML→PDF)
 * y refinar fidelidad con IA.
 */
public interface PdfEditorUseCase {

    /**
     * Convierte un PDF en un documento editable (HTML + estructura), enriqueciéndolo con IA.
     */
    Mono<PdfEditorDocument> importPdf(byte[] pdfBytes, String bearerToken);

    /**
     * Regenera el PDF a partir del HTML editado por página.
     */
    Mono<byte[]> exportPdf(PdfEditorExportRequest request);

    /**
     * Refina la fidelidad del documento editable usando IA.
     */
    Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(PdfEditorRefineRequest request, String bearerToken);
}
