package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnoreProperties;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonInclude;

import java.io.Serializable;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Una página editable del documento PDF.
 *
 * @param pageNumber número de página (1-based)
 * @param widthPt    ancho de la página en puntos (pt)
 * @param heightPt   alto de la página en puntos (pt)
 * @param html       HTML editable completo de la página (posicionamiento absoluto fiel)
 * @param elements   vista estructurada de los elementos (para edición semántica y para la IA)
 */
@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
@JsonInclude(JsonInclude.Include.NON_NULL)
public record PdfEditorPage(
        int pageNumber,
        float widthPt,
        float heightPt,
        String html,
        List<PdfEditorElement> elements
) implements Serializable {
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElement;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.TreeMap;

/**
 * Construye el HTML editable (posicionamiento absoluto, unidades en pt) a partir de la lista
 * estructurada de elementos. Se comparte entre la conversión determinística (PdfBoxToHtmlAdapter)
 * y la capa de IA (que regenera el HTML tras reconstruir tablas).
 */
public final class PdfEditorHtmlBuilder {

    private PdfEditorHtmlBuilder() {
    }

    public static String buildPageHtml(
            final int pageNumber, final float widthPt, final float heightPt,
            final List<PdfEditorElement> elements) {

        final StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(4096);
        sb.append("<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset=\"UTF-8\"/><style>");
        sb.append("@page{size:").append(formatPt(widthPt)).append("pt ")
                .append(formatPt(heightPt)).append("pt;margin:0;}");
        sb.append("*{box-sizing:border-box;}");
        sb.append("body{margin:0;padding:0;}");
        sb.append(".pdf-page{position:relative;width:").append(formatPt(widthPt)).append("pt;height:")
                .append(formatPt(heightPt)).append("pt;overflow:hidden;background:#ffffff;}");
        sb.append(".el{position:absolute;margin:0;padding:0;}");
        sb.append("</style></head><body><div class=\"pdf-page\" data-page=\"")
                .append(pageNumber).append("\">");
        for (PdfEditorElement element : elements) {
            sb.append(buildElementHtml(element));
        }
        sb.append("</div></body></html>");
        return sb.toString();
    }

    public static String buildElementHtml(final PdfEditorElement element) {
        switch (element.type()) {
            case TEXT, TABLE_CELL -> {
                final StringBuilder style = new StringBuilder(basePositionStyle(element));
                style.append("width:").append(formatPt(element.width())).append("pt;")
                        .append("height:").append(formatPt(element.height())).append("pt;")
                        .append("white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;");
                appendStyle(style, element.style());
                final String semantic = element.semanticType() == null ? ""
                        : " data-semantic=\"" + escapeHtml(element.semanticType()) + "\"";
                return "<div class=\"el\" data-id=\"" + element.id() + "\" data-type=\"text\"" + semantic
                        + " style=\"" + style + "\">" + escapeHtml(element.text()) + "</div>";
            }
            case IMAGE -> {
                final StringBuilder style = new StringBuilder(basePositionStyle(element));
                style.append("width:").append(formatPt(element.width())).append("pt;")
                        .append("height:").append(formatPt(element.height())).append("pt;");
                return "<img class=\"el\" data-id=\"" + element.id() + "\" data-type=\"image\" style=\""
                        + style + "\" src=\"" + (element.src() == null ? "" : element.src()) + "\"/>";
            }
            case RECT -> {
                final StringBuilder style = new StringBuilder(basePositionStyle(element));
                style.append("width:").append(formatPt(element.width())).append("pt;")
                        .append("height:").append(formatPt(element.height())).append("pt;");
                appendStyle(style, element.style());
                return "<div class=\"el\" data-id=\"" + element.id() + "\" data-type=\"rect\" style=\""
                        + style + "\"></div>";
            }
            case TABLE -> {
                return buildTableHtml(element);
            }
            default -> {
                return "";
            }
        }
    }

    private static String buildTableHtml(final PdfEditorElement table) {
        final List<PdfEditorElement> cells = table.children() == null ? List.of() : table.children();

        final TreeMap<Integer, List<PdfEditorElement>> rows = new TreeMap<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement cell : cells) {
            rows.computeIfAbsent(Math.round(cell.y()), k -> new ArrayList<>()).add(cell);
        }

        final StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append("<div class=\"el\" data-id=\"").append(table.id()).append("\" data-type=\"table\" style=\"")
                .append(basePositionStyle(table))
                .append("width:").append(formatPt(table.width())).append("pt;")
                .append("height:").append(formatPt(table.height())).append("pt;\">");
        sb.append("<table style=\"width:100%;height:100%;border-collapse:collapse;\">");
        for (List<PdfEditorElement> row : rows.values()) {
            row.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(PdfEditorElement::x));
            sb.append("<tr>");
            for (PdfEditorElement cell : row) {
                sb.append("<td style=\"").append(buildCellStyle(cell)).append("\">")
                        .append(escapeHtml(cell.text())).append("</td>");
            }
            sb.append("</tr>");
        }
        sb.append("</table></div>");
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static String buildCellStyle(final PdfEditorElement cell) {
        final StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("border:0.5pt solid #000000;vertical-align:middle;padding:0;");
        appendStyle(sb, cell.style());
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static String basePositionStyle(final PdfEditorElement element) {
        return "left:" + formatPt(element.x()) + "pt;top:" + formatPt(element.y()) + "pt;";
    }

    private static void appendStyle(final StringBuilder sb, final Map<String, String> style) {
        if (style == null) {
            return;
        }
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : style.entrySet()) {
            final String property = toCssProperty(entry.getKey());
            String value = entry.getValue();
            if (value == null) {
                continue;
            }
            if (("font-size".equals(property) || "letter-spacing".equals(property))
                    && !value.endsWith("pt")) {
                value = value + "pt";
            }
            sb.append(property).append(':').append(value).append(';');
        }
    }

    private static String toCssProperty(final String key) {
        return switch (key) {
            case "fontFamily" -> "font-family";
            case "fontSize" -> "font-size";
            case "fontWeight" -> "font-weight";
            case "fontStyle" -> "font-style";
            case "letterSpacing" -> "letter-spacing";
            case "lineHeight" -> "line-height";
            case "textAlign" -> "text-align";
            case "backgroundColor" -> "background-color";
            case "borderColor" -> "border-color";
            case "borderWidth" -> "border-width";
            case "borderStyle" -> "border-style";
            default -> key;
        };
    }

    public static String formatPt(final float value) {
        if (Math.abs(value - Math.round(value)) < 0.001f) {
            return String.valueOf(Math.round(value));
        }
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", value);
    }

    private static String escapeHtml(final String text) {
        if (text == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return text.replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;");
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/PdfVisionRouter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Primary;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfVisionPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Selecciona el proveedor de IA del editor en tiempo de arranque según
 * {@code application.pdf.editor.ai-provider} (valores: {@code openai}, {@code neuroia}, {@code none}).
 *
 * <p>Es el único bean {@code @Primary} de {@link PdfVisionPort}, por lo que {@link pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.service.PdfEditorService}
 * inyecta siempre este router sin ambigüedad.</p>
 */
@Primary
@Component
public class PdfVisionRouter implements PdfVisionPort {

    private final PdfVisionPort delegate;

    public PdfVisionRouter(
            @Qualifier("openAiPdfVisionAdapter") final PdfVisionPort openAi,
            @Qualifier("neuroIaPdfVisionAdapter") final PdfVisionPort neuroIa,
            @Qualifier("noOpPdfVisionAdapter") final PdfVisionPort noOp,
            @Value("${application.pdf.editor.ai-provider:openai}") final String provider) {

        this.delegate = switch (provider.toLowerCase()) {
            case "neuroia" -> neuroIa;
            case "none", "off", "noop" -> noOp;
            default -> openAi;
        };
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> enhance(final PdfEditorDocument document, final String bearerToken) {
        return delegate.enhance(document, bearerToken);
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(
            final PdfEditorDocument document, final List<String> screenshots, final String bearerToken) {
        return delegate.refine(document, screenshots, bearerToken);
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/NeuroIaPdfVisionAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfVisionPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElement;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElementType;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorPage;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.external.AiClassificationResponse;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.PdfEditorHtmlBuilder;
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Proveedor de IA basado en NeuroIA (servicio interno).
 *
 * <p>{@code enhance} clasifica semánticamente cada elemento de texto (tipo: heading, amount,
 * table-header, ...) usando {@link AiLayoutClientAdapter#callNeuroIaToClassify} y anota el resultado
 * en {@code PdfEditorElement.semanticType}, regenerando el HTML con {@code data-semantic}.</p>
 *
 * <p>{@code refine} (comparación visual) requiere un puente hacia {@code PdfEditableStructure},
 * pendiente de implementar.</p>
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class NeuroIaPdfVisionAdapter implements PdfVisionPort {

    private final AiLayoutClientAdapter aiLayoutClientAdapter;

    @Value("${application.pdf.editor.neuroia.prompt-code:PDF_LAYOUT_AI_GEOMETRY_COMPILER_V2}")
    private String promptCode;

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> enhance(final PdfEditorDocument document, final String bearerToken) {
        if (document == null || document.pages() == null || document.pages().isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
        }

        return Flux.fromIterable(document.pages())
                .flatMapSequential(page -> enhancePage(page, bearerToken))
                .collectList()
                .map(pages -> new PdfEditorDocument(document.documentId(), pages, document.metadata()))
                .onErrorReturn(document);
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(
            final PdfEditorDocument document, final List<String> screenshots, final String bearerToken) {
        // El refinamiento visual de NeuroIA trabaja sobre PdfEditableStructure (bridge pendiente).
        return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
    }

    private Mono<PdfEditorPage> enhancePage(final PdfEditorPage page, final String bearerToken) {
        final List<PdfEditorElement> words = page.elements() == null
                ? List.of()
                : page.elements().stream()
                        .filter(e -> e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.TEXT)
                        .toList();

        if (words.isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.just(page);
        }

        final List<Map<String, String>> minimalData = words.stream()
                .map(w -> Map.of("id", w.id(), "text", w.text() == null ? "" : w.text()))
                .toList();

        return aiLayoutClientAdapter.callNeuroIaToClassify(minimalData, promptCode, bearerToken)
                .map(classifications -> applySemantic(page, classifications))
                .onErrorReturn(page);
    }

    private PdfEditorPage applySemantic(
            final PdfEditorPage page,
            final List<AiClassificationResponse.ClassificationItem> classifications) {
        if (classifications == null || classifications.isEmpty()) {
            return page;
        }

        final Map<String, String> typeById = new HashMap<>();
        for (AiClassificationResponse.ClassificationItem item : classifications) {
            typeById.put(item.id(), item.type());
        }

        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement e : page.elements()) {
            final String type = typeById.get(e.id());
            if (type != null && e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.TEXT) {
                elements.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                        e.id(), e.type(), e.x(), e.y(), e.width(), e.height(),
                        e.text(), e.style(), e.src(), e.children(), type));
            } else {
                elements.add(e);
            }
        }

        final String html = PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.buildPageHtml(
                page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), elements);
        return new PdfEditorPage(page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), html, elements);
    }
}


===================================================
FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/external/OpenAiPdfVisionAdapter.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonIgnoreProperties;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.reactive.function.client.WebClient;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfVisionPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElement;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorElementType;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorPage;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.PdfEditorHtmlBuilder;
import reactor.core.publisher.Flux;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Mejora del documento editable usando OpenAI gpt-4o.
 *
 * <p>Su tarea principal es la <b>reconstrucción de tablas</b>: recibe las palabras de cada página
 * (con coordenadas) y devuelve regiones de tabla con celdas, que se integran al documento
 * sustituyendo las palabras correspondientes y regenerando el HTML.</p>
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
public class OpenAiPdfVisionAdapter implements PdfVisionPort {

    private static final String OPENAI_URL = "https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions";
    private static final Duration TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(30);

    private static final String SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
            Eres un experto en análisis de layout de documentos PDF.
            Recibes las palabras de una página con sus coordenadas (x, y, w, h en puntos, origen
            arriba-izquierda) y las dimensiones de la página.
            Detecta las regiones que forman tablas (filas y columnas alineadas) y agrupa las palabras
            de cada región en celdas por fila y columna.
            Devuelve estrictamente JSON con el siguiente esquema:
            { "tables": [ { "x": number, "y": number, "width": number, "height": number,
                            "cells": [ { "x": number, "y": number, "width": number, "height": number,
                                         "text": string } ] } ] }
            Si no hay tablas, devuelve { "tables": [] }.
            Las coordenadas deben usar las mismas unidades (puntos). No añadas explicaciones.
            """;

    private static final String REFINE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
            Eres un experto en fidelidad visual de documentos PDF.
            Recibes la captura de una página y la lista de sus elementos de texto con posiciones actuales
            (x, y, width, height y fontSize, en puntos, origen arriba-izquierda).
            Compara la captura con las posiciones y devuelve las correcciones necesarias para que el texto
            coincida visualmente con la imagen.
            Devuelve estrictamente JSON con el esquema:
            { "corrections": [ { "id": string, "x": number, "y": number, "width": number,
                                  "height": number, "fontSize": number } ] }
            Solo incluye los elementos que necesitan corrección. Si todo coincide, devuelve { "corrections": [] }.
            No añadas explicaciones.
            """;

    private static final String OCR_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
            Eres un motor OCR de alta precisión. Recibes la imagen de una página escaneada y sus
            dimensiones en puntos. Extrae todo el texto visible y devuelve cada línea con su posición.
            Devuelve estrictamente JSON con el esquema:
            { "lines": [ { "text": string, "x": number, "y": number, "width": number, "height": number } ] }
            (x, y = esquina superior izquierda; width/height en puntos; origen arriba-izquierda).
            Si no hay texto, devuelve { "lines": [] }. No añadas explicaciones.
            """;

    private final WebClient webClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    @Value("${openai.api.key:default-secret-key}")
    private String openAiApiKey;

    @Value("${application.pdf.editor.openai.model:gpt-4o}")
    private String model;

    public OpenAiPdfVisionAdapter(final WebClient.Builder builder, final ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.webClient = builder.build();
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> enhance(final PdfEditorDocument document, final String bearerToken) {
        if (document == null || document.pages() == null || document.pages().isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
        }

        return Flux.fromIterable(document.pages())
                .flatMapSequential(this::enhancePage)
                .collectList()
                .map(pages -> new PdfEditorDocument(document.documentId(), pages, document.metadata()))
                .onErrorReturn(document);
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(
            final PdfEditorDocument document, final List<String> screenshots, final String bearerToken) {
        if (document == null || document.pages() == null || document.pages().isEmpty()) {
            return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
        }
        final List<String> shots = screenshots == null ? List.of() : screenshots;

        return Flux.fromIterable(document.pages())
                .flatMapSequential(page -> refinePage(page, shotFor(page, shots)))
                .collectList()
                .map(pages -> new PdfEditorDocument(document.documentId(), pages, document.metadata()))
                .onErrorReturn(document);
    }

    private String shotFor(final PdfEditorPage page, final List<String> shots) {
        final int index = page.pageNumber() - 1;
        return (index >= 0 && index < shots.size()) ? shots.get(index) : null;
    }

    private Mono<PdfEditorPage> refinePage(final PdfEditorPage page, final String screenshot) {
        if (screenshot == null || screenshot.isBlank()) {
            return Mono.just(page);
        }
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> buildRefineContent(page))
                .flatMap(content -> callVision(content, screenshot))
                .map(corrections -> applyCorrections(page, corrections))
                .onErrorReturn(page);
    }

    private Mono<List<Correction>> callVision(final String refineContent, final String screenshot) {
        final Map<String, Object> textPart = new HashMap<>();
        textPart.put("type", "text");
        textPart.put("text", refineContent);

        final Map<String, Object> imageUrl = new HashMap<>();
        imageUrl.put("url", screenshot);

        final Map<String, Object> imagePart = new HashMap<>();
        imagePart.put("type", "image_url");
        imagePart.put("image_url", imageUrl);

        final Map<String, Object> systemMessage = new HashMap<>();
        systemMessage.put("role", "system");
        systemMessage.put("content", REFINE_SYSTEM_PROMPT);

        final Map<String, Object> userMessage = new HashMap<>();
        userMessage.put("role", "user");
        userMessage.put("content", List.of(textPart, imagePart));

        final Map<String, Object> body = new HashMap<>();
        body.put("model", model);
        body.put("messages", List.of(systemMessage, userMessage));
        body.put("response_format", Map.of("type", "json_object"));

        return webClient.post()
                .uri(OPENAI_URL)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + openAiApiKey)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(body)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(OpenAiResponse.class)
                .timeout(TIMEOUT)
                .flatMap(this::parseCorrections);
    }

    private Mono<List<Correction>> parseCorrections(final OpenAiResponse response) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            if (response == null || response.choices() == null || response.choices().isEmpty()) {
                return List.<Correction>of();
            }
            final String content = response.choices().get(0).message().content();
            if (content == null || content.isBlank()) {
                return List.<Correction>of();
            }
            final RefineResult result = objectMapper.readValue(content, RefineResult.class);
            return result.corrections() == null ? List.<Correction>of() : result.corrections();
        }).onErrorResume(e -> {
            log.warn("No se pudo interpretar las correcciones de OpenAI: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Mono.just(List.<Correction>of());
        });
    }

    private String buildRefineContent(final PdfEditorPage page) throws JsonProcessingException {
        final List<Map<String, Object>> elementList = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement e : page.elements()) {
            if (e.type() != PdfEditorElementType.TEXT) {
                continue;
            }
            final Map<String, Object> m = new HashMap<>();
            m.put("id", e.id());
            m.put("text", e.text() == null ? "" : e.text());
            m.put("x", e.x());
            m.put("y", e.y());
            m.put("width", e.width());
            m.put("height", e.height());
            m.put("fontSize", e.style() != null ? e.style().getOrDefault("fontSize", "10") : "10");
            elementList.add(m);
        }
        final Map<String, Object> payload = new HashMap<>();
        payload.put("pageWidth", page.widthPt());
        payload.put("pageHeight", page.heightPt());
        payload.put("elements", elementList);
        return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(payload);
    }

    private PdfEditorPage applyCorrections(final PdfEditorPage page, final List<Correction> corrections) {
        if (corrections == null || corrections.isEmpty()) {
            return page;
        }
        final Map<String, Correction> byId = new HashMap<>();
        for (Correction c : corrections) {
            if (c.id() != null) {
                byId.put(c.id(), c);
            }
        }

        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement e : page.elements()) {
            final Correction c = byId.get(e.id());
            if (c != null && e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.TEXT) {
                final Map<String, String> style =
                        new LinkedHashMap<>(e.style() == null ? Map.of() : e.style());
                if (c.fontSize() != null && c.fontSize() > 0) {
                    style.put("fontSize", PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.formatPt(c.fontSize()));
                }
                elements.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                        e.id(), e.type(),
                        c.x() != null ? c.x() : e.x(),
                        c.y() != null ? c.y() : e.y(),
                        c.width() != null ? c.width() : e.width(),
                        c.height() != null ? c.height() : e.height(),
                        e.text(), style, e.src(), e.children(), e.semanticType()));
            } else {
                elements.add(e);
            }
        }

        final String html = PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.buildPageHtml(
                page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), elements);
        return new PdfEditorPage(page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), html, elements);
    }

    private Mono<PdfEditorPage> enhancePage(final PdfEditorPage page) {
        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = page.elements() == null ? List.of() : page.elements();
        final List<PdfEditorElement> words = elements.stream()
                .filter(e -> e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.TEXT)
                .toList();

        if (words.isEmpty()) {
            // Posible página escaneada (sin capa de texto): OCR sobre la imagen de fondo.
            return elements.stream()
                    .filter(e -> e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.IMAGE && e.src() != null)
                    .findFirst()
                    .map(image -> ocrPage(page, image.src()))
                    .orElseGet(() -> Mono.just(page));
        }

        return detectTables(page, words)
                .map(tables -> mergeTables(page, tables))
                .onErrorReturn(page);
    }

    private Mono<PdfEditorPage> ocrPage(final PdfEditorPage page, final String imageDataUri) {
        return callOcr(imageDataUri, page)
                .map(boxes -> applyOcr(page, boxes))
                .onErrorReturn(page);
    }

    private Mono<List<OcrBox>> callOcr(final String imageDataUri, final PdfEditorPage page) {
        final Map<String, Object> textPart = new HashMap<>();
        textPart.put("type", "text");
        textPart.put("text", "pageWidth: " + PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.formatPt(page.widthPt())
                + ", pageHeight: " + PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.formatPt(page.heightPt()));

        final Map<String, Object> imageUrl = new HashMap<>();
        imageUrl.put("url", imageDataUri);
        final Map<String, Object> imagePart = new HashMap<>();
        imagePart.put("type", "image_url");
        imagePart.put("image_url", imageUrl);

        final Map<String, Object> systemMessage = new HashMap<>();
        systemMessage.put("role", "system");
        systemMessage.put("content", OCR_SYSTEM_PROMPT);

        final Map<String, Object> userMessage = new HashMap<>();
        userMessage.put("role", "user");
        userMessage.put("content", List.of(textPart, imagePart));

        final Map<String, Object> body = new HashMap<>();
        body.put("model", model);
        body.put("messages", List.of(systemMessage, userMessage));
        body.put("response_format", Map.of("type", "json_object"));

        return webClient.post()
                .uri(OPENAI_URL)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + openAiApiKey)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(body)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(OpenAiResponse.class)
                .timeout(TIMEOUT)
                .flatMap(this::parseOcr);
    }

    private Mono<List<OcrBox>> parseOcr(final OpenAiResponse response) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            if (response == null || response.choices() == null || response.choices().isEmpty()) {
                return List.<OcrBox>of();
            }
            final String content = response.choices().get(0).message().content();
            if (content == null || content.isBlank()) {
                return List.<OcrBox>of();
            }
            final OcrResult result = objectMapper.readValue(content, OcrResult.class);
            return result.lines() == null ? List.<OcrBox>of() : result.lines();
        }).onErrorResume(e -> {
            log.warn("No se pudo interpretar el OCR de OpenAI: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Mono.just(List.<OcrBox>of());
        });
    }

    private PdfEditorPage applyOcr(final PdfEditorPage page, final List<OcrBox> lines) {
        if (lines == null || lines.isEmpty()) {
            return page;
        }
        // Conserva la imagen de fondo (el escaneo) y superpone el texto OCR editable.
        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>(page.elements());
        for (OcrBox line : lines) {
            if (line.text() == null || line.text().isBlank()) {
                continue;
            }
            elements.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                    UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                    PdfEditorElementType.TEXT,
                    line.x() != null ? line.x() : 0f,
                    line.y() != null ? line.y() : 0f,
                    line.width() != null ? line.width() : 0f,
                    line.height() != null ? line.height() : 0f,
                    line.text(), Map.of("fontSize", "10"), null, null, null));
        }
        final String html = PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.buildPageHtml(
                page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), elements);
        return new PdfEditorPage(page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), html, elements);
    }

    private Mono<List<DetectedTable>> detectTables(
            final PdfEditorPage page, final List<PdfEditorElement> words) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> buildUserContent(page, words))
                .flatMap(this::callOpenAi)
                .onErrorResume(e -> {
                    log.warn("OpenAI no pudo analizar la página {}: {}", page.pageNumber(), e.getMessage());
                    return Mono.just(List.of());
                })
                .defaultIfEmpty(List.of());
    }

    private Mono<List<DetectedTable>> callOpenAi(final String userContent) {
        final Map<String, Object> body = Map.of(
                "model", model,
                "messages", List.of(
                        Map.of("role", "system", "content", SYSTEM_PROMPT),
                        Map.of("role", "user", "content", userContent)),
                "response_format", Map.of("type", "json_object"));

        return webClient.post()
                .uri(OPENAI_URL)
                .header(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, "Bearer " + openAiApiKey)
                .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .bodyValue(body)
                .retrieve()
                .bodyToMono(OpenAiResponse.class)
                .timeout(TIMEOUT)
                .flatMap(this::parseContent);
    }

    private Mono<List<DetectedTable>> parseContent(final OpenAiResponse response) {
        return Mono.fromCallable(() -> {
            if (response == null || response.choices() == null || response.choices().isEmpty()) {
                return List.<DetectedTable>of();
            }
            final String content = response.choices().get(0).message().content();
            if (content == null || content.isBlank()) {
                return List.<DetectedTable>of();
            }
            final TableDetectionResult result = objectMapper.readValue(content, TableDetectionResult.class);
            return result.tables() == null ? List.<DetectedTable>of() : result.tables();
        }).onErrorResume(e -> {
            log.warn("No se pudo interpretar la respuesta de tablas de OpenAI: {}", e.getMessage());
            return Mono.just(List.<DetectedTable>of());
        });
    }

    private String buildUserContent(final PdfEditorPage page, final List<PdfEditorElement> words)
            throws JsonProcessingException {
        final List<Map<String, Object>> wordList = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement w : words) {
            final Map<String, Object> m = new HashMap<>();
            m.put("id", w.id());
            m.put("text", w.text() == null ? "" : w.text());
            m.put("x", w.x());
            m.put("y", w.y());
            m.put("w", w.width());
            m.put("h", w.height());
            wordList.add(m);
        }

        final Map<String, Object> payload = new HashMap<>();
        payload.put("pageWidth", page.widthPt());
        payload.put("pageHeight", page.heightPt());
        payload.put("words", wordList);
        return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(payload);
    }

    private PdfEditorPage mergeTables(final PdfEditorPage page, final List<DetectedTable> tables) {
        if (tables == null || tables.isEmpty()) {
            return page;
        }

        final List<PdfEditorElement> elements = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PdfEditorElement e : page.elements()) {
            if (e.type() == PdfEditorElementType.TEXT && insideAnyTable(e, tables)) {
                continue; // la palabra queda absorbida por una tabla
            }
            elements.add(e);
        }

        for (DetectedTable table : tables) {
            final List<PdfEditorElement> cells = new ArrayList<>();
            for (DetectedCell cell : table.cells()) {
                cells.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                        UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                        PdfEditorElementType.TABLE_CELL,
                        cell.x(), cell.y(), cell.width(), cell.height(),
                        cell.text(), Map.of("fontSize", "10"), null, null, null));
            }
            elements.add(new PdfEditorElement(
                    UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                    PdfEditorElementType.TABLE,
                    table.x(), table.y(), table.width(), table.height(),
                    null, Map.of(), null, cells, null));
        }

        final String html = PdfEditorHtmlBuilder.buildPageHtml(
                page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), elements);
        return new PdfEditorPage(page.pageNumber(), page.widthPt(), page.heightPt(), html, elements);
    }

    private boolean insideAnyTable(final PdfEditorElement element, final List<DetectedTable> tables) {
        final float cx = element.x() + element.width() / 2f;
        final float cy = element.y() + element.height() / 2f;
        for (DetectedTable table : tables) {
            if (cx >= table.x() && cx <= table.x() + table.width()
                    && cy >= table.y() && cy <= table.y() + table.height()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // =====================================================================================
    // Modelos de respuesta
    // =====================================================================================

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record OpenAiResponse(List<Choice> choices) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record Choice(Message message) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record Message(String content) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record TableDetectionResult(List<DetectedTable> tables) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record DetectedTable(float x, float y, float width, float height, List<DetectedCell> cells) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record DetectedCell(float x, float y, float width, float height, String text) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record RefineResult(List<Correction> corrections) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record Correction(String id, Float x, Float y, Float width, Float height, Float fontSize) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record OcrResult(List<OcrBox> lines) {
    }

    @JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = true)
    private record OcrBox(String text, Float x, Float y, Float width, Float height) {
    }
}

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound.external;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.outbound.PdfVisionPort;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Implementación sin IA: devuelve el documento determinístico sin cambios.
 * Usada cuando {@code application.pdf.editor.ai-provider=none}.
 */
@Component
public class NoOpPdfVisionAdapter implements PdfVisionPort {

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> enhance(final PdfEditorDocument document, final String bearerToken) {
        return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
    }

    @Override
    public Mono<PdfEditorDocument> refine(
            final PdfEditorDocument document, final List<String> screenshots, final String bearerToken) {
        return Mono.justOrEmpty(document);
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/inbound/rest/PdfEditorController.java

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.inbound.rest;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.core.io.buffer.DataBufferUtils;
import org.springframework.http.ContentDisposition;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.http.codec.multipart.FilePart;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestHeader;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestPart;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorExportRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.dto.PdfEditorRefineRequest;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.application.port.inbound.PdfEditorUseCase;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.PdfEditorDocument;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.common.RespBase;
import reactor.core.publisher.Mono;

/**
 * Endpoints del editor PDF (pipeline PDF → HTML editable → PDF).
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/private/v1/pdf/editor")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PdfEditorController {

    private static final String DEFAULT_EXPORT_NAME = "edited.pdf";

    private final PdfEditorUseCase pdfEditorUseCase;

    /**
     * Importa un PDF y devuelve su representación editable (HTML + estructura), enriquecida con IA.
     */
    @PostMapping(value = "/import", consumes = MediaType.MULTIPART_FORM_DATA_VALUE)
    public Mono<RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>> importPdf(
            @RequestPart("file") Mono<FilePart> filePart,
            @RequestHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION) String bearerToken) {
        return filePart
                .flatMap(this::readBytes)
                .flatMap(bytes -> pdfEditorUseCase.importPdf(bytes, bearerToken))
                .map(result -> new RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>().ok(result));
    }

    /**
     * Exporta el HTML editado a un único PDF descargable.
     */
    @PostMapping(value = "/export", produces = MediaType.APPLICATION_PDF_VALUE)
    public Mono<ResponseEntity<byte[]>> exportPdf(@RequestBody PdfEditorExportRequest request) {
        return pdfEditorUseCase.exportPdf(request)
                .map(bytes -> ResponseEntity.ok()
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_PDF)
                        .header(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_DISPOSITION,
                                ContentDisposition.attachment().filename(resolveFileName(request)).build().toString())
                        .contentLength(bytes.length)
                        .body(bytes));
    }

    /**
     * Refina la fidelidad del documento editable mediante IA.
     */
    @PostMapping("/refine")
    public Mono<RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>> refine(
            @RequestBody PdfEditorRefineRequest request,
            @RequestHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION) String bearerToken) {
        return pdfEditorUseCase.refine(request, bearerToken)
                .map(result -> new RespBase<PdfEditorDocument>().ok(result));
    }

    private Mono<byte[]> readBytes(FilePart filePart) {
        return DataBufferUtils.join(filePart.content())
                .map(dataBuffer -> {
                    byte[] bytes = new byte[dataBuffer.readableByteCount()];
                    dataBuffer.read(bytes);
                    DataBufferUtils.release(dataBuffer);
                    return bytes;
                });
    }

    private String resolveFileName(PdfEditorExportRequest request) {
        if (request != null && request.fileName() != null && !request.fileName().isBlank()) {
            String name = request.fileName().trim().replaceAll("[\\\\/:*?\"<>|\\s]+", "_");
            return name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf") ? name : name + ".pdf";
        }
        return DEFAULT_EXPORT_NAME;
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/PdfPageRenderer.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.apache.pdfbox.pdmodel.PDDocument;
import org.apache.pdfbox.rendering.PDFRenderer;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Base64;

@Slf4j
@Component
public class PdfPageRenderer {

    private static final float RENDER_DPI = 150f;

    public String renderPageToBase64Png(PDDocument doc, int pageIndex) throws IOException {
        if (doc == null || pageIndex < 0 || pageIndex >= doc.getNumberOfPages()) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            PDFRenderer renderer = new PDFRenderer(doc);
            BufferedImage image = renderer.renderImageWithDPI(pageIndex, RENDER_DPI);
            if (image == null) {
                return null;
            }
            ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            if (!ImageIO.write(image, "png", out)) {
                return null;
            }
            return "data:image/png;base64," + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(out.toByteArray());
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            log.warn("No se pudo renderizar la página {} a PNG: {}", pageIndex + 1, e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }
}

FILE: src/main/java/pillihuaman/com/pe/engine/infrastructure/adapter/outbound/PdfToCssTranslator.java
===================================================

package pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.infrastructure.adapter.outbound;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.TextElement;
import pillihuaman.com.pe.engine.domain.model.TranslatedElement;

@Component
public class PdfToCssTranslator {

    private static final float PT_TO_PX = 1.333333f;
    private static final float ASCENT_FACTOR = 0.75f;
    private static final float BOX_HEIGHT_RATIO = 1.3f;
    private static final float LINE_HEIGHT_RATIO = 1.15f;

    public TranslatedElement translate(TextElement raw, float pageHeightPt) {
        float cssFontSize = raw.fontSize() * PT_TO_PX;

        // CORRECCIÓN: raw.top() ya es Top-Down (0 = borde superior).
        // Solo lo pasamos a píxeles y le restamos el ajuste de la fuente.
        float cssTop = (raw.top() * PT_TO_PX) - (cssFontSize * ASCENT_FACTOR);

        float cssLeft = raw.left() * PT_TO_PX;
        float cssWidth = raw.width() * PT_TO_PX;
        float cssHeight = cssFontSize * BOX_HEIGHT_RATIO;
        float cssLineHeight = cssFontSize * LINE_HEIGHT_RATIO;

        return new TranslatedElement(
                raw.id(),
                raw.text(),
                "text",
                cssLeft,
                cssTop,
                cssWidth,
                cssHeight,
                cssFontSize,
                cssLineHeight,
                raw.fontName(),
                raw.fontWeight(),
                raw.isItalic(),
                raw.resourceReference()
        );
    }
}
